# Field catalogue

Generated by `node scripts/field-catalogue.cjs` from `native/artifacts/native.d.ts`, the binding's readers, the adapter's sources, the contracts in `scripts/field-contracts/`, the source record in `docs/field-evidence.json` and the packed layouts: do not edit. `test/field-catalogue.test.cjs` fails when an exported field has no entry here, when an entry is vague or cites nothing, and when a packed name changes without this page.

The binding exports 90 interfaces with 936 members, and 141 functions and methods. A stable reference is the heading of the type and the name of the member, as `docs/field-catalogue.md#nativesimulationconfig` and `gravity`.

## How to read a row

- **type**: as declared. **Rust**: what the binding holds it as. Every number crosses as a JavaScript double. A row narrowed to `f32` is a value vanilla holds as a float: the reader rounds it, deliberately, as vanilla would.
- **absent**: `required` means the type needs it. Otherwise the reader's own expression: what a field left out reads as. Left out is not `0` or `false` unless the expression says so (`docs/state-contract.md`, Omission). `left out when it has no value` is a result's field.
- **unit**: from the contract. `flag` and `name` are what the type settles.
- **legal range**: a whole-number field refuses a fraction, a value past its type, `NaN` and infinity by name, where N-API alone would truncate or wrap it. A public position, size, box or config value that is not finite or is out of range is refused before anything changes, with the field's name on the player and the state's on the other families (`docs/numeric-contract.md`). A range narrower than the type is given where the code or vanilla sets it.
- **owner and timing**: `caller` keeps it and sends it each call. `engine, seeded` is the engine's between ticks: a caller seeds it and must hand back what it read, not a guess (`docs/state-contract.md`, Owners). `one tick` is an event the next tick clears. The player's rows are that page's matrix.
- **releases**: where the row can mean something: the family's range (below) cut by the row's own bound. The bound is the row's own `since` or `until` with the line that shows it, a row of `docs/capability-table.md`, or where vanilla added or dropped the cited member when that is the mechanic's own boundary (`MECHANIC_ANCHORS` in `scripts/field-contracts.cjs`). Where the member is, by identity, is read in every named source from 1.8.8 (the six legacy trees remapped to 1.14.4's names, then every Mojang-named client) and followed through renames by Fabric's intermediary ids (`scripts/field-lineage.cjs`, `docs/field-evidence.json`), and where no mapping can follow it, read in the tree of the release beside the edge (`scripts/field-read-edges.cjs`): `‡ member A..B` says the member is only there in A to B, and before or after it the mechanic, if any, lives in another member. `named so` in the source column is only when the member had that name: a rename bounds nothing. `†` marks a lower bound no source could check: below the release shown the member's identity is lost (a legacy mapping left it unnamed, or an override's name changed where no id follows it). The binding takes a field on any release its family runs on; on a release outside the row's range vanilla has nothing there and it reads as inert.
- **meaning**: what the value is and what reads or writes it. **source**: the vanilla member and a line of the newest release that holds it, or the rule of the binding that owns a value vanilla has no member for.
- **direction**: `in` is read when the call starts and stays the caller's. `out` is produced by the call and is the engine's. `in and out` is both. `none` is an interface no entry point names.
- **adapter types** have no Rust column: they are the caller's own objects. Their `releases` is the release the adapter's engine was made for.

## Families

Measured on the addon by `FAMILY_PROBES`: the first and last registered release an entry point of the family runs on.

| family | releases | note |
|---|---|---|
| player | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | the player's tick |
| boat | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | the boat's tick |
| mount | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | a ridden mob's tick, each kind from `entityTravelSupport` |
| minecart | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | a cart's tick |
| moving-entity | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | an item's tick; each kind checks its own release |
| projectile | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | a snowball's tick; each kind checks its own release (`docs/projectile-capabilities.md`) |
| arrow | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | an arrow's tick |
| trident | 1.13.2 to 26.3 | a trident's tick |
| dragon | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | the dragon's tick |
| shulker | 1.11.2 to 26.3 | a shulker box's tick |
| mob-travel | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | a travelling mob's tick, each kind from `entityTravelSupport` |
| piston | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | a piston's push |
| world | 1.8.8 to 26.3 | a block update |

## Examples

Omitted versus zero attributes, and carrying persistent outputs into later ticks: `docs/state-contract.md`, Examples, for the player, and `docs/field-maps.md` for what each state keeps. Both ideas on a ridden mob, run by `test/doc-examples.test.cjs`:

```js doctest
const world = new binding.NativeWorld();
const horse = { position: { x: 0.5, y: 100, z: 0.5 }, velocity: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, yaw: 0, forward: 1, strafe: 0, jumpRequested: false, onGround: false, inWater: false };
// omitted is the release's gravity, an explicit 0 is none
assert.ok(binding.simulateVersionedHorseTick('1.21.5', world, horse).velocity.y < 0);
assert.equal(binding.simulateVersionedHorseTick('26.3', world, { ...horse, gravity: 0 }).velocity.y, 0);
```

A tick's result is the next tick's input: the kept fields go on, and a state rebuilt by hand starts them over.

```js doctest
let carried = { ...horse, boosting: true, boostTime: 0, boostTimeTotal: 100, entityType: 'pig' };
for (let tick = 0; tick < 3; tick += 1) carried = binding.simulateVersionedHorseTick('1.21.5', world, carried);
assert.equal(carried.boostTime, 3);
assert.equal(binding.simulateVersionedHorseTick('1.21.5', world, { ...carried, boostTime: undefined }).boostTime, 1);
```

### NativeAabb

Direction: in and out.

Releases: 1.8.8 to 26.3, the player's tick.

| member | type | Rust | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `min` | `NativeVec3` | `Vec3` | required |  |  | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | corner with the lowest x, y and z of an absolute or cell-relative box, as the holding field says | `AABB.minX`, 26.3 world/phys/AABB.java:15, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |
| `max` | `NativeVec3` | `Vec3` | required |  |  | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | corner with the highest x, y and z of the box; From<NativeAabb> copies both corners unchecked | `AABB.maxX`, 26.3 world/phys/AABB.java:18, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |

### NativeAttributeModifier

One modifier of an attribute: its amount and operation (add_value, add_multiplied_base or add_multiplied_total).

Direction: in and out.

Releases: 1.8.8 to 26.3, the player's tick.

| member | type | Rust | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `amount` | `number` | `f64` | required | attribute value |  | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | amount the modifier adds, or scales by, in MovementSpeedAttribute.value | `AttributeModifier.amount or getAmount`, 26.3 world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier.java:14, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |
| `operation` | `string` | `String` | required | name |  | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | add_value, add_multiplied_base or add_multiplied_total; TryFrom refuses any other name | `AttributeModifier.operation or getOperation`, 26.3 world/entity/ai/attributes/AttributeModifier.java:14, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |

### NativeBoatPlacement

Direction: out.

| `yawDegrees` | `number` | `f64` | required | degrees |  | engine: produced by the call | 1.8.8..26.3 | Its yaw, the placer's | `BoatItem.use`, 26.3 world/item/BoatItem.java:30, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |

### NativeBoatVehicle

The boat's observed geometry and status at its passenger's fluid-query phase. This input affects fluid queries only while `passenger` is true. Callers own updates between ticks; a batch does not predict the boat's movement.

Direction: in and out.

Releases: 1.8.8 to 26.3, the boat's tick.

| member | type | Rust | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `boundingBox` | `NativeAabb` | `Aabb` | required |  |  | caller, the tick's scene: read for this tick's passenger fluid query | 1.8.8..26.3 | The boat's absolute world-space bounding box, not the passenger's box. | `Entity.getBoundingBox`, 26.3 world/entity/Entity.java:3558, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |
| `underwater` | `boolean` | `bool` | required | flag |  | caller, the tick's scene: read for this tick's passenger fluid query | 1.8.8..26.3 ‡ member 1.16..26.3 | Boat status UNDER_WATER or UNDER_FLOWING_WATER, not ordinary IN_WATER. | `Boat or AbstractBoat.isUnderWater`, 26.3 world/entity/vehicle/boat/AbstractBoat.java:775, named so 1.16 to 26.3 |

### NativeBuildIdentity

What this binary is, as it was compiled: the Rust target and the hash of the sources it was built from (native/build.rs). native/artifacts/provenance.*.json records the same, so a binary from another build cannot pass as this one, and a WebAssembly fallback cannot pass as a native addon.

Direction: out.

Releases: 1.8.8 to 26.3, a block update.

| member | type | Rust | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `target` | `string` | `String` | required | name |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | Rust target triple the addon was compiled for, which native/artifacts/provenance records too | binding: build_identity reads MCPN_BUILD_TARGET from native/build.rs |
| `sourceHash` | `string` | `String` | required | name |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | SHA-256 over the hashed listing of Cargo.toml, Cargo.lock, build.rs, data and src, line endings normalized | binding: build_identity reads MCPN_SOURCE_HASH from native/build.rs |
| `crateVersion` | `string` | `String` | required | name |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | version of the native crate at compile time | binding: build_identity reads CARGO_PKG_VERSION |

### NativeGeneratedStateShape

A channel of a block state as the release's own code answers it (scripts/java-oracle/ShapeDump.java). `status` is `exact` (the boxes are the answer, none being an answer too), `contextual` (the answer also needs what `depends_on` lists, so there are no boxes) or `absent` (the release has no such method).

Direction: out.

Releases: 1.8.8 to 26.3, a block update.

| member | type | Rust | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `status` | `string` | `String` | required | name |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | exact (the boxes are the answer, none included), contextual (needs dependsOn, no boxes) or absent (no such method) | binding: native_state_shape names the StateShape from scripts/java-oracle/ShapeDump.java |
| `dependsOn` | `Array<string>` | `Vec<String>` | required |  |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | what a contextual answer also needs: position, level or entity; empty otherwise | binding: native_state_shape reads the DEPENDS_ mask bits |
| `boxes` | `Array<NativeGeneratedShapeBox>` | `Vec<GeneratedShapeBox>` | required |  |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | boxes of the channel for an exact status, empty otherwise | binding: native_state_shape copies the exact boxes |

### NativeMinecartExplosion

A TNT cart's Level.explode and the blocks its rays reach, air included, as the explosion's toBlow has them: `certain` breaks under any of the level's draws, `possible` under some, and `blown` is the exact list where the rules gave `levelRandom`, with `levelRandom` after it. Breaking them is the server's, after the entities.

### NativeMinecartRules

What the level's enabled features select. `improvements` is required: a release alone does not say (1.21.2+ has both paths). `maxMinecartSpeed` is the game rule, required with the improvements. `minY` is the level's bottom (Level.getMinY), required of a cart so low that it decides, from 1.18 where it is no longer 0. `tntExplodes` is the game rule of 1.21.5+.

Direction: in.

Releases: 1.8.8 to 26.3, a cart's tick.

| member | type | Rust | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `improvements` | `boolean` | `bool` | required | flag |  | caller: read when the call starts | 1.21.2..26.3 | The level has minecart_improvements, which picks the improved path over the old; refused before 1.21.2 | `AbstractMinecart.useExperimentalMovement`, 26.3 world/entity/vehicle/minecart/AbstractMinecart.java:536, named so 1.21.2 to 26.3 |
| `maxMinecartSpeed` | `number` | `i32` | an Option to `rules_of`: absent stays apart from any value | blocks a second | 1..1000 | caller: read when the call starts | 1.21.2..26.3 | The game rule, the improved path's top speed; required with the improvements, else ignored | `GameRules.RULE_MINECART_MAX_SPEED or MAX_MINECART_SPEED`, 26.3 world/level/gamerules/GameRules.java:51, named so 1.21.2 to 26.3 |
| `minY` | `number` | `i32` | read by `rules_of` | blocks | whole, a Java int | caller: read when the call starts | 1.8.8..26.3 ‡ member 1.17..26.3 | Level.getMinY: a cart 64 under it is gone; 0 when absent, and refused from 1.18 for a cart under y -64 | `LevelHeightAccessor.getMinBuildHeight or getMinY`, 26.3 world/level/LevelHeightAccessor.java:9, named so 1.17 to 26.3 |
| `tntExplodes` | `boolean` | `bool` | `unwrap_or(true)` in `rules_of` | flag |  | caller: read when the call starts | 1.21.5..26.3 | The game rule, read from 1.21.5: false keeps a fuse from priming and a blast from going off; true when absent | `GameRules.RULE_TNT_EXPLODES or TNT_EXPLODES`, 26.3 world/level/gamerules/GameRules.java:79, named so 1.21.5 to 26.3 |
| `netherExit` | `boolean` | `bool` | `unwrap_or(true)` in `rules_of` | flag |  | caller: read when the call starts | 1.8.8..26.3 | A nether portal has a portal of the level's beyond it: false leaves a cart only the cooldown of the attempt; true when absent | binding: Scene::go_through reads it |

### NativeTickOutcome

| `autoSpinAttackEnded` | `boolean` | `bool` | required | flag |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 ‡ member 1.13.2..26.3 | riptide spin attack ended this tick (checkAutoSpinAttack) | `LivingEntity.checkAutoSpinAttack`, 26.3 world/entity/LivingEntity.java:3188, named so 1.13.2 to 26.3 |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `string` | `String` | required | name |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | vehicleBeforeMove or vehicleAfterMove, the head or the tail of the move | binding: move_vehicle_among's emit_vehicle_move names the two |
| `position` | `NativeVec3` | `Vec3` | required |  |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | vehicle's position at that point | `Entity.position or x`, 26.3 world/entity/Entity.java:223, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |
| `velocity` | `NativeVec3` | `Vec3` | required |  |  | engine: produced by the call, read when it returns | 1.8.8..26.3 | vehicle's velocity at that point | `Entity.getDeltaMovement or velocityX`, 26.3 world/entity/Entity.java:3851, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |

### NativeVersionedEntityState

A living entity of simulateVersionedEntityTicks.

Direction: in and out.

Releases: 1.8.8 to 26.3, the player's tick.

| member | type | Rust | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `simulation` | `NativeSimulationState` | `SimulationState` | required |  |  | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | simulation state of the entity, moved and handed back by EntityBatch | binding: EntityBatch::states hands it back |
| `noPhysics` | `boolean` | `bool` | required | flag |  | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | cancels every Entity.push with this entity | `Entity.noPhysics`, 26.3 world/entity/Entity.java:248, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |
| `id` | `number` | `u32` | read in the binding's own loop | id | whole, 0..2^32-1 | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | id other entities' vehicleId name, which marks passengers of one vehicle | `Entity.getId`, 26.3 world/entity/Entity.java:407, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |
| `vehicleId` | `number` | `u32` | read in the binding's own loop | id | whole, 0..2^32-1 | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 ‡ member 1.9.4..26.3 | id of the entity it rides, which keeps the two from pushing each other | `Entity.getVehicle`, 26.3 world/entity/Entity.java:3749, named so 1.9.4 to 26.3 |
| `pushable` | `boolean` | `bool` | required | flag |  | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..26.3 | Entity.isPushable: a push reaches it only if true, and from 1.19 it takes one only if true | `Entity.isPushable`, 26.3 world/entity/Entity.java:2081, named so 1.8.8 to 26.3 |
| `pushthrough` | `number` | `f64` | required | ratio | 0..1 | caller seeds it, the engine returns it: hand back what it gave | 1.8.8..1.16.5 | Entity.pushthrough before 1.17: the pusher's scales the push by 1 minus it; refused outside 0..1 | `Entity.pushthrough`, 1.16.5 world/entity/Entity.java:150, named so 1.8.8 to 1.16.5 |

## Packed layouts

Layout version 3: 32 input slots, 24 output slots, 21 numbers per entity. `nativePlayerLayout()` says what the addon was built with. A flag value is a bit of the `FLAGS` slot. A name with no state field here has no one-to-one field in `scripts/state-bridge.cjs`, which is the mapping. `test/state-bridge.test.cjs` holds that mapping to these names.

### NativePlayerInput

| `POSITION` | 0 | `position` |
| `VELOCITY` | 3 | `velocity` |
| `YAW` | 6 | `yaw` |
| `PITCH` | 7 | `pitch` |
| `LOOK` | 8 | `look` |
| `STRAFE` | 11 | `input` |
| `FORWARD` | 12 |  |

| `FLAGS` | 16 |  |
| `FALL_DISTANCE` | 17 | `fallDistance` |
| `JUMP_TICKS` | 18 | `jumpTicks` |
| `AUTO_JUMP_TIME` | 19 | `autoJumpTime` |

| entry point | takes | gives | note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NativeBulkRun.players` | `string`, `Array<NativeSimulationState>`, `NativeSimulationConfig` | `NativeBulkRun` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.entities` | `string`, `Array<NativeVersionedEntityState>`, `NativeSimulationConfig` | `NativeBulkRun` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.boats` | `string`, `Array<NativeBoatState>` | `NativeBulkRun` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.horses` | `string`, `Array<NativeHorseState>` | `NativeBulkRun` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.projectiles` | `string`, `Array<NativeProjectileState>` | `NativeBulkRun` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.arrows` | `string`, `Array<NativeArrowState>` | `NativeBulkRun` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.tridents` | `string`, `Array<NativeTridentState>` | `NativeBulkRun` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.advance` | `NativeWorld`, `number` | `void` | Runs `ticks` more whole ticks in `world`, all of them before it returns. |
| `NativeBulkRun.dispose` |  | `void` | Frees the states. Advancing or reading the run after this refuses. |
| `NativeBulkRun.playerStates` |  | `Array<NativeSimulationState>` | The players after the ticks so far, each with the scene it has reached. |
| `NativeBulkRun.entityStates` |  | `Array<NativeVersionedEntityState>` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.boatStates` |  | `Array<NativeBoatState>` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.horseStates` |  | `Array<NativeHorseState>` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.projectileResults` |  | `Array<NativeProjectileTickResult>` | The result of the last tick, as simulateVersionedProjectileTicks gives: empty before one. |
| `NativeBulkRun.arrowResults` |  | `Array<NativeArrowTickResult>` |  |
| `NativeBulkRun.tridentResults` |  | `Array<NativeTridentTickResult>` |  |
| `NativePlayer.constructor` | `string`, `NativeSimulationState`, `NativeSimulationConfig` | `NativePlayer` |  |
| `NativePlayer.setState` | `NativeSimulationState` | `void` | Replaces the whole state (its nearby entities too, when it has them): what the last tick reported, a tick tickMove left unfinished and the world it ticked in are the old state's, and go with it. A state that is refused changes nothing. |
| `NativePlayer.state` |  | `NativeSimulationState` | The whole state after the last tick or push. |
| `NativePlayer.setBoundingBox` | `NativeAabb \| undefined \| null` | `void` | Replaces the bounding box kept before 1.17 (Entity.setBoundingBox: a refitted size). |
| `NativePlayer.tick` | `NativeWorld`, `Float64Array`, `Float64Array` | `Float64Array` | One tick: the input array's values go into the state, the tick runs, and its results come back laid out as `output`. `entities` holds the nearby entities of this tick (ENTITY_LENGTH numbers each, in the order the client ticks them). |
| `NativePlayer.tickMove` | `NativeWorld`, `Float64Array`, `Float64Array` | `Float64Array` | `tick` up to Player.tick's pose update (26.2 Player.java:266): the rockets and entities before the player and its move. The caller chooses the pose where the move left the player, and finishTick runs what ticks after the player with the box of that pose. |
| `NativePlayer.finishTick` | `NativeWorld`, `number` | `Float64Array` | What ticks after the player in a tick tickMove started: the rockets and entities after it, which meet the box `height` tall, the pose update's, as the block entities' pushes then do. The results come back laid out as `output`, and entityPushes tells the whole tick's. Without a tickMove before it there is no tick to finish, which fails and changes nothing. |
| `NativePlayer.pushByShulkerBox` | `NativeWorld`, `NativeShulkerBox`, `number`, `number`, `number` | `Float64Array` | What an opening shulker box's lid at a cell does to the player, after its tick as the block entities tick after the entities; the state comes back laid out as `output`. |
| `NativePlayer.entityPushes` |  | `Float64Array` | The pushes the nearby entities took back from the player in the last tick (Entity.push: the other way), three numbers each: the entity's index in the entities array, then x and z. |
| `NativePlayer.pushByMovingPistons` | `NativeWorld`, `Array<NativeMovingPiston>` | `Float64Array` | The pushes of moving pistons after the tick (simulateVersionedTick's movingPistons), each call after the others of the same tick; the results come back laid out as `output`. |
| `NativeWorld.constructor` |  | `NativeWorld` |  |
| `NativeWorld.checkpoint` |  | `Buffer` | The whole world as bytes, for nativeWorldFromCheckpoint: equal worlds give equal bytes. |
| `NativeWorld.contentDigest` |  | `string` | What names this world's content in a checkpoint. |
| `NativeWorld.setRelease` | `string` | `void` | The release whose client this world is: what it reads in a chunk it does not hold of the shared empty chunk, which keeps what is written there (WorldSnapshot::set_release; 1.8 its blocks, 1.13 their fluids, other releases nothing). A world given none reads nothing there. |
| `NativeWorld.setChunkLoaded` | `number`, `number`, `boolean` | `void` |  |
| `NativeWorld.isChunkLoaded` | `number`, `number` | `boolean` |  |
| `NativeWorld.setWorldBounds` | `number`, `number`, `number`, `number`, `number`, `number \| undefined \| null` | `void` |  |
| `NativeWorld.setMovingBlock` | `string`, `NativeMovingPiston`, `Array<NativeAabb> \| undefined \| null` | `Array<NativeAabb>` | MovingPistonBlock.getCollisionShape: the block at the piston's cell takes the shape its block entity gives at its progress, which a move before the block entity's tick meets. `base_shapes`: the extended base a source piston leaves as it retracts. Returns the boxes the cell collides with, relative to it (free to leave it), for the caller's own collision queries. |
| `NativeWorld.clearMovingBlock` | `number`, `number`, `number` | `void` | The moving block's block entity ended: the block has no shape until it is written anew. |
| `NativeWorld.isInsideWorldBorder` | `number`, `number`, `string \| undefined \| null` | `boolean` | WorldBorder.isWithinBounds(x, z) of the release (the newest one when none is given). |
| `NativeWorld.noBorderCollision` | `NativeVec3`, `NativeVec3`, `NativeVec3`, `NativeVec3`, `number`, `number`, `string \| undefined \| null` | `boolean` | CollisionGetter.noBorderCollision of the release from 1.14 (the newest one when none is given): whether the world border leaves `query` free for an entity at x, z with box `bounds`. A release before 1.14 has no such term: its border cells are in the block query. |

| `nativePlayerLayout` |  | `NativePlayerLayout` | The packed arrays this build reads and writes, for a caller that keeps them apart from it. |
| `nativeWorldFromCheckpoint` | `Buffer` | `NativeWorld` | A world from the bytes of NativeWorld.checkpoint. It is a new world: a NativePlayer that ticked in another takes it through setState. |
| `projectileTargetHitbox` | `NativeVec3`, `number`, `number`, `number \| undefined \| null` | `NativeAabb` | The box of an entity of `width` and `height` scaled by `scale`, as `boundingBox` wants it. |
| `refreshVersionedPose` | `string`, `NativeWorld`, `NativeSimulationState`, `number`, `number`, `boolean`, `number \| undefined \| null` | `NativePoseRefreshResult` | Player.updatePlayerPose (updateSize before 1.17): the requested pose when it fits, else crouching or swimming, and no change without room to swim; a spectator or passenger takes the requested pose unchecked (1.14.4 Player.java:333-363, 1.21.11 Player.java:336-352). Each fit is CollisionGetter.noCollision for the player (its block context and the state's nearbyEntities that collide) of the pose's box, shrunk by 1e-7 from 1.16 (1.14.4 Entity.java:1769- 1643, 1.15 :1651-1653, 1.16 :1687-1689, 1.21.11 Player.java:368-370). `scale` is the SCALE attribute, read from 1.20.5. |
| `setMathJava` | `number \| undefined \| null` | `void` | Makes every release follow one Java major's Math.sin and Math.cos (8, or 9 and up, which agree), or with null each the one its version json declares: Java 8 up to 1.16.5 (crate::math::math_java). For the whole process; the recorder runs every release on Java 21 or 25, so its replays set 21. |

## Adapter types

The interfaces the TypeScript side declares beside the binding. The adapter reads and writes their fields on the caller's objects.

### BulkRunOptions

| member | type | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `chunkTicks` | `number` | allowed | ticks |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | ticks of a chunk, the most a cancellation waits for times the entities; at least 1, DEFAULT_CHUNK_TICKS (20) by default | binding: runBulk in src/bulk.ts shrinks it to fit maxChunkWork |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal` | allowed |  |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | stops the run between two chunks; a run already stopped runs nothing | binding: runBulk in src/bulk.ts polls signal.aborted before each chunk |
| `onProgress` | `void` | allowed |  |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | called after each chunk with the ticks the run has done in all and those of this call | binding: runBulk in src/bulk.ts calls it after run.advance |

Declared in `src/bulk.ts`.

### BulkRunOutcome

| member | type | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ticksDone` | `number` | required | ticks |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | whole ticks of the run, this call's and those before | binding: runBulk in src/bulk.ts reads run.ticksDone |
| `ticksRun` | `number` | required | ticks |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | whole ticks of this call | binding: runBulk in src/bulk.ts adds up the chunks |
| `cancelled` | `boolean` | required | flag |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | signal stopped this call short of its ticks, leaving the run ready to go on | binding: runBulk in src/bulk.ts sets it at a chunk boundary |

Declared in `src/bulk.ts`.

| member | type | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |

| member | type | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |

| member | type | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `number` | required | id |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | The entity id, the key of MinecartTracker.carts, from spawn_entity or adopt | binding: MinecartTracker.spawned takes it from the packet |

| `velocity` | `NativeVec3` | required | blocks a tick |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | The client's cart motion: tick writes it on the improved path, the packets and lerpTo on the old | binding: MinecartTracker.velocity and lerpTo write it |
| `yRot` | `number` | required | degrees |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | The client's cart yaw, written by tick and by the packets through byteAngle | binding: MinecartTracker.tick and lerpTo write it |

| `onGround` | `boolean` | required | flag |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | The last move packet's onGround, kept when a packet gives none; no tick here reads it | binding: MinecartTracker.moved, looked, teleported and synced write it |

### PrismarineWorld

A prismarine world: mineflayer's bot.world, or anything that gives the prismarine block at a position.

| member | type | absent | unit | legal range | owner and timing | releases | meaning | source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `getBlock` | `PrismarineBlock \| null \| undefined` | required |  |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | prismarine block at a position, read when a state id does not answer | binding: NativeWorldSync in src/mineflayer/world.ts calls it |
| `getSkyLight` | `number \| null \| undefined` | allowed | count | 0..15 | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | sky light at a position; rain needs 15, so a world without it has no rain | binding: rainingAt in src/mineflayer/world.ts calls it |
| `getBlockStateId` | `number \| null \| undefined` | allowed | id |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | state id at a position, read without building a block | binding: NativeWorldSync in src/mineflayer/world.ts calls it |

| `on` | `unknown` | allowed |  |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | a world that has it gets listeners for blockUpdate and chunk columns, and only changed blocks are read again; others are read whole each tick | binding: NativeWorldSync in src/mineflayer/world.ts attaches them |
| `off` | `unknown` | allowed |  |  | adapter: held on the caller's object | as the release's engine | takes those listeners off, preferred over removeListener; a world with neither keeps them, which then do nothing | binding: NativeWorldSync.detach in src/mineflayer/world.ts calls it |

### MovingBlock

A PistonMovingBlockEntity as a move meets it: its cell, its Direction.from3DDataValue, the block whose shape it moves (at this progress: the head a source piston shows, else the block it moves) and the extended base a source piston leaves as it retracts.
